## 4.2 `boundary_conditions.d` 鐨勫熀鏈粨鏋?
`boundary_conditions.d` 鐢变竴缁?`patch` 缁勬垚銆傛瘡涓?patch 鎻忚堪涓€绫昏竟鐣屽尯鍩燂紝姣斿 `main_air`銆乣methane_ring`銆乣outlet`銆乣external_adiabatic_walls`銆?

```text
version 1;

patch main_air
{
  kind inlet;
  type fixedVelocityInlet;
  enabled true;
  allowEmpty false;
  priority 10;

  selector
  {
    source externalBoundary;
    side west;
    marker any;
    region all;
  }

  targetMarker -1;
  U fixedValue (0 8.0 0);
  T fixedValue 295;
  p fixedValue 100000;

  composition volumeFraction
  {
    O2 0.21;
    N2 0.79;
    normalize true;
  }
}
```

## 4.7 鐢茬兎/绌烘皵杈圭晫绀轰緥

涓嬮潰鏄竴涓畝鍖栫殑鐢茬兎/绌烘皵绀轰緥锛岄噸鐐瑰睍绀哄啓娉曘€傜湡瀹炵畻渚嬪簲浠ヨ嚜宸辩殑鍧愭爣銆佸崐寰勩€乻ide 鍜岀幇鏈夋枃浠舵敞閲婁负鍑嗐€?
鍋囪锛?
- 涓绘祦鏂瑰悜鏄 `y`锛?- 绌烘皵浠庝綆 `y` 澶栭儴杈圭晫杩涘叆锛?- 鐢茬兎浠庝竴涓?IBM 鍑犱綍鍦嗙幆鍠峰叆锛?- 鍑哄彛鍦ㄩ珮 `y` 澶栭儴杈圭晫锛?- 绌烘皵娓╁害鍜岀敳鐑锋俯搴﹂兘鏄?`295 K`锛?- 鍙傝€冨帇鍔涙槸 `100000 Pa`銆?
```text
version 1;

patch main_air
{
  kind inlet;
  type fixedVelocityInlet;
  enabled true;
  allowEmpty false;
  priority 10;

  selector
  {
    source externalBoundary;
    side west;
    marker any;
    region cylinderRing;
    axis y;
    center (0 0);
    radiusRange (0.0 0.03);
    axialCoordinate y;
    axialRange (-1.0e30 1.0e30);
  }

  targetMarker -1;
  U fixedValue (0 8.0 0);
  T fixedValue 295;
  p fixedValue 100000;

  composition volumeFraction
  {
    O2 0.21;
    N2 0.79;
    normalize true;
  }
}

patch methane_ring
{
  kind inlet;
  type fixedVelocityInlet;
  enabled true;
  allowEmpty false;
  priority 20;

  selector
  {
    source ibmSurface;
    side all;
    marker any;
    region cylinderRing;
    axis y;
    center (0 0);
    radiusRange (0.0069 0.0081);
    axialCoordinate y;
    axialRange (-0.00825 -0.00775);
  }

  targetMarker -1;
  U model wallNormalIntoFluid;
  U fixedValue (40.0 0 0);
  T fixedValue 295;
  p fixedValue 100000;

  composition volumeFraction
  {
    CH4 1.0;
    normalize true;
  }
}

patch outlet
{
  kind outlet;
  type relaxedPressureOutlet;
  enabled true;
  allowEmpty false;
  priority 10;

  selector
  {
    source externalBoundary;
    side east;
    marker any;
    region all;
  }

  targetMarker -2;
  p fixedValue 100000;

  backflow
  {
    normalFraction 0.1;
    tangentialDamping 0.8;
    scalarBlend 0.1;
    T fixedValue 295;

    composition volumeFraction
    {
      O2 0.21;
      N2 0.79;
      normalize true;
    }
  }
}

patch external_adiabatic_walls
{
  kind wall;
  type noSlipAdiabaticWall;
  enabled true;
  priority 5;

  selector
  {
    source externalBoundary;
    side all;
    markers (-5 -50);
    region all;
  }

  targetMarker -5;
  U fixedValue (0 0 0);
  thermal adiabatic;
  species zeroGradient;
}
```

杩欎釜渚嬪瓙閲屾湁涓や釜鍏ュ彛锛?
- `main_air` 鏄┖姘斿叆鍙ｏ紝浼樺厛绾?`10`锛?- `methane_ring` 鏄敳鐑峰渾鐜叆鍙ｏ紝浼樺厛绾?`20`锛屽鏋滃畠鍜屾洿瀹界殑绌烘皵鍖哄煙閲嶅彔锛屼細鍚庡簲鐢ㄣ€?
娉ㄦ剰锛氱ず渚嬮噷鐨?`side west/east`銆佸崐寰勫拰 `y` 鑼冨洿涓嶆槸閫氱敤甯告暟銆傛崲绠椾緥鏃跺繀椤绘牴鎹嚑浣曞拰棰勮璋冩暣銆?
